This work introduces a novel 3D iterative mesh refinement framework that balances anatomical and geometric forces derived from inherent anatomical rules to refine an initial, possibly ambiguous, segmentation into a high-precision, anatomically plausible result. Designed as a model-agnostic post-processing step, our method uses a 3D vector field to iteratively push the vertices to the correct anatomical locations. Evaluating the refinement on both a high-resolution in-house dataset and a coarse, sparsely annotated open-source dataset, our method consistently improves all volumetric, surface, and anatomical metrics. The framework demonstrates greater improvement when applied to weaker initial segmentations, highlighting its potential for improving segmentations for out-of-domain models and in limited-training-data scenarios. The method is formulated as a gradient-based, GPU-accelerated framework that can be easily extended to other anatomical use cases.

</details>


### [70] [IGGT4D: Streaming 4D Instance-Grounded Geometry Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19228)
*Zhengyu Zou,Hao Li,Kuixuan Jiao,Liu Liu,Tingyang Xiao,Xiaolin Zhou,Fangzhou Hong,Zhizhong Su,Dingwen Zhang,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IGGT4D，一种流式实例引导几何Transformer，用于在线4D场景理解。它通过因果时空建模顺序处理视频帧，增量更新相机运动、几何和物体身份的联合表示，实现长序列前馈重建与几何-实例一致性。同时构建了InsScene4D-147K大规模数据集，在3D重建、位姿估计、实例空间跟踪和开放词汇分割等任务上优于现有流式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式3D重建方法主要关注几何，缺乏时序一致的物体级理解；而语义重建和3D视觉-语言方法依赖外部2D语义线索或松散耦合的几何输入，限制了长动态场景中几何与实例的统一学习。

Method: IGGT4D采用流式Transformer架构，顺序处理视频帧，通过因果时空建模复用历史上下文，增量更新相机运动、几何和物体身份的联合表示。同时构建InsScene4D-147K数据集，使用自动化几何引导标注流水线生成时序一致的实例掩码。

Result: 在3D重建、位姿估计、实例空间跟踪和开放词汇分割任务上，IGGT4D优于现有流式基线，并保持长动态序列的可扩展在线推理能力。

Conclusion: IGGT4D通过统一的流式框架实现了几何与实例的联合学习，解决了长动态场景中在线4D理解的挑战，高质量数据集InsScene4D-147K进一步推动了该领域的发展。

Abstract: Real-world spatial intelligence requires agents to understand scenes from continuous video streams, where objects move, persist, disappear, and reappear over time. While recent spatial foundation models have enabled generalizable feed-forward 3D reconstruction, most streaming methods remain geometry-centric and lack temporally consistent object-level understanding. Meanwhile, existing semantic reconstruction and 3D-aware vision-language methods largely rely on externally extracted 2D semantic cues or loosely coupled geometry inputs, limiting unified geometry-instance learning in long dynamic scenes. In this paper, we propose IGGT4D, a streaming instance-grounded geometry Transformer for online 4D scene understanding. IGGT4D processes video frames sequentially, reuses historical context through causal spatial-temporal modeling, and incrementally updates a unified representation of camera motion, geometry, and object identity. This enables long-sequence feed-forward reconstruction with geometry-instance consistency in dynamic environments. To address the lack of high-quality 4D supervision, we further construct InsScene4D-147K, a large-scale dataset spanning real/synthetic and static/dynamic scenes, with RGB images, depth, poses, and temporally consistent instance masks generated by an automated geometry-guided annotation pipeline. Experiments on 3D reconstruction, pose estimation, instance spatial tracking, and open-vocabulary segmentation demonstrate that IGGT4D outperforms existing streaming baselines while maintaining scalable online inference for long dynamic sequences.

</details>


### [71] [PathAgentBench: Benchmarking Evidence-Seeking Vision-Language Models on Whole-Slide Pathology Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19261)
*Dankai Liao,Tianyi Zhang,Yufeng Wu,Xinyue Zhang,Qiaochu Xue,Zeyu Liu,Dachun Zhao,Linghan Cai,Yueming Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PathAgentBench是一个评估视觉语言模型在千兆像素全切片图像中主动寻找和整合诊断证据能力的基准，包含1,822张TCGA WSI和17,135条由十位认证病理学家注释的诊断路径。实验发现，尽管模型在多尺度推理和跨模态匹配任务上表现良好，但在诊断区域定位（最佳mIoU低于0.09）和自主探索（高倍率下命中率仅0.020）方面存在显著不足，揭示了模型在从WSI直接获取证据与利用已有证据推理之间存在巨大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有病理学基准大多在预裁剪的图块或预提取的切片特征上评估模型，未能测试模型直接从千兆像素全切片图像中获取证据的能力，因此需要建立一个统一的基准来全面评估视觉语言模型在WSI诊断中的证据寻求能力。

Method: PathAgentBench构建了一个诊断树结构，将不同放大倍率下的嵌套区域与尺度特定的发现和路径级诊断相连接，涵盖四个互补能力：图像到文本匹配、文本到图像检索、诊断区域定位和多尺度推理。基准包含1,822张TCGA WSI和17,135条诊断路径，以及一个额外的190张乳腺癌WSI私有队列用于评估自主全切片探索。

Result: 领先的开源模型在多尺度推理上达到93%以上准确率，跨模态匹配任务上超过50%准确率。但诊断区域定位最具挑战性，最佳文本引导mIoU低于0.09，甚至不如简单的中心启发式方法。自主探索中，无条件命中率从低倍率的0.522下降到中倍率的0.185和高倍率的0.020。

Conclusion: PathAgentBench揭示出当前VLM在处理经过整理的证据时表现良好，但从WSI直接获取证据的能力存在明显差距，为衡量和改进证据寻求型病理模型提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Whole-slide image (WSI) diagnosis requires identifying diagnostically relevant regions, examining them across magnifications, and integrating multi-scale evidence. However, most existing pathology benchmarks evaluate models on pre-cropped patches or pre-extracted slide features, leaving their ability to acquire evidence directly from gigapixel WSIs largely untested. We introduce PathAgentBench, a benchmark for evaluating evidence-seeking vision-language models (VLMs) across four complementary capabilities: image-to-text matching for evidence interpretation, text-to-image retrieval for evidence verification, diagnostic-region localization for evidence acquisition, and multi-scale reasoning for evidence integration. The benchmark is organized as a diagnostic tree that links nested regions across magnifications with scale-specific findings and path-level diagnoses. It contains 1,822 TCGA WSIs and 17,135 diagnostic paths annotated by ten board-certified pathologists. An additional private cohort of 190 breast cancer WSIs with detailed annotations is used to evaluate autonomous whole-slide exploration. We evaluate 20 general-purpose, medical, and pathology-specialized models. Leading open-weight models achieve over 93% accuracy in multi-scale reasoning and over 50% accuracy in both cross-modal matching tasks. In contrast, diagnostic-region localization remains challenging: the best text-guided mean intersection-over-union is below 0.09, underperforming a simple center-based heuristic. During autonomous exploration, the unconditional hit rate decreases from 0.522 at low magnification to 0.185 at intermediate magnification and 0.020 at high magnification. These results reveal a pronounced gap between reasoning over curated evidence and acquiring that evidence directly from WSIs. PathAgentBench provides a unified framework for measuring and improving evidence-seeking pathology models.

</details>


### [72] [No Training, Better Flights: Test-Time Scaled VLMs for UAV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19288)
*Feinan Cheng,Dongliang Xu,Wenli Nong,Zhiheng Zhang,Ang Liu,Tianyu Wang,Yue Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需额外训练的测试时扩展方法，通过迭代细化、多候选生成、自修正及多标准评分，提升无人机视觉语言导航模型的推理精度与安全性，达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机视觉语言导航方法通常依赖单次推理，在复杂环境中易产生次优或不安全的轨迹，缺乏深层次规划能力。

Method: 1) 提示模型生成多个并行候选路径；2) 执行自修正步骤，重评估初始规划；3) 设计多标准评分函数（安全性、目标对齐、前进进度）评估细化后的候选方案。

Result: 在无人机视觉语言导航任务上达到SOTA性能，生成了更准确可靠的飞行计划。

Conclusion: 测试时扩展结合多候选生成与自修正，能有效提升冻结VLMs的导航规划能力，无需额外训练即可实现更强的鲁棒性与安全性。

Abstract: Test-time scaling offers a promising method to improve the inference performance of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) without additional training. Existing approaches to vision-language navigation (VLN) for Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) typically relies on a single inference pass, which can falter in complex environments by producing suboptimal or unsafe trajectories. In this paper, we explore a simple and effective approach to apply test-time scaling to VLN for UAV. We enhance navigation reasoning through an iterative refinement process that requires no extra model training, guiding the model to re-evaluate its initial navigation plan for better accuracy and safety. Our method first prompts the model to generate multiple parallel candidates and then performs a self-correction step, achieving deeper and more robust planning without changing the underlying model. To further strengthen decision-making, we design a multi-criteria scoring function to evaluate the refined candidates based on safety, goal alignment, and forward-progress. This simple yet powerful combination enables a frozen UAV navigation VLMs to self-correct and generate more accurate and reliable flight plans, achieving SOTA performance in this task.

</details>


### [73] [ERank in Latent Space as an Image-Complexity and Richness Measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19315)
*Maksim Smirnov,Grigory Kononov,Anastasiia Linich,Egor Surkov,Egor Shvetsov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出有效秩（ERank）作为衡量图像视觉丰富度的无标签指标，通过预训练编码器的单次前向传播计算深度特征通道协方差的秩，发现其与码率、清晰度、边缘密度及人类复杂度标注相关，并验证了在超分辨率和OCR任务中作为数据筛选标准的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉丰富度度量依赖人工标注或复杂计算，缺乏一个简单、无监督且与下游任务性能相关的指标。

Method: 定义ERank为深度特征图通道协方差矩阵的有效秩，通过单次前向传播从冻结的预训练编码器中计算，统计图像激活的去相关通道方向数量。

Result: ERank与码率比特率、清晰度、边缘密度及IC9600人类复杂度标注相关性达r=0.72；去除低ERank样本提升超分辨率性能，去除高ERank样本提升OCR性能，但对分类、分割、去噪无帮助。

Conclusion: ERank是一种廉价且有效的视觉丰富度信号，当任务难度由输入丰富度主导时尤为有用。

Abstract: We propose the effective rank (ERank) of the channel covariance of an image's deep feature map as a per-sample, label-free measure of visual richness, computed from a single forward pass through a frozen pretrained encoder. ERank counts how many decorrelated channel directions an image activates, and we characterize its properties, including its behavior under noise. Empirically, ERank orders images from plain to visually rich, correlates with codec bitrate, sharpness, and edge density, and correlates with human complexity annotations on IC9600 with $r = 0.72$. As a data-selection criterion, removing low-ERank samples improves super-resolution and removing high-ERank samples improves OCR, in both pretraining and finetuning, while selection does not help classification, segmentation, or denoising. ERank is thus a cheap richness signal, useful exactly when task difficulty is governed by input richness.

</details>


### [74] [InstructMixup: Instruction-Guided Salient Patch Editing for Robust Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19324)
*Khawar Islam,Arif Mahmood,Xin Jin,Naveed Akhtar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出InstructMixup，一种仅在单样本内部进行数据增强的方法，通过轻量级显著性检测器提取多尺度显著块，利用指令引导生成模型编辑后融合回非显著区域，并在显著区域注入自适应比例的自相似分形结构，从而构建标签一致且具有挑战性的训练样本。该方法在七个基准测试、多种骨干网络（CNN、ViT、VLM）上均超越九种竞争方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的混合式数据增强方法（mixup-based）在计算信息性混合区域时开销大，且跨样本混合常破坏语义完整性，因此需要一种既能生成具有挑战性的样本又能保持标签一致性的高效数据增强方法。

Method: 1) 使用轻量级显著性检测器提取多尺度显著块；2) 通过指令引导生成模型对每个显著块进行编辑并离线缓存；3) 将编辑后的块融合回同一样本的非显著区域；4) 在显著区域以自适应比例注入自相似分形结构。训练时生成编辑无需重复计算。

Result: 在七个基准测试上，InstructMixup在粗粒度/细粒度分类、对损坏/遮挡的鲁棒性、校准、迁移学习及自监督学习等任务中，均优于九种竞争数据增强方法。

Conclusion: InstructMixup通过单样本内生成编辑与分形结构注入，实现了标签一致且具有挑战性的数据增强，同时推导了邻域风险的二阶近似，验证了该方法在保持生成编辑不变性及抑制扰动方向曲率方面的有效性。

Abstract: In image and video technologies, data augmentation is widely used to improve the generalization of deep visual models, and mixup-based strategies that interpolate between samples have become the dominant approach. However, computing informative mixing regions adds substantial overhead, and blending content across different images frequently disrupts the semantic integrity of the resulting sample. We propose \our{}, a data augmentation method that constructs challenging yet label-consistent training samples entirely within a single visual sample. \our{} first extracts multi-scale salient patches from the sample using a lightweight saliency detector, refines each patch with an instruction-guided generative model, and blends the edited patch back into the non-salient regions of the same sample; because the generative edits are computed once and cached offline, this step adds negligible training cost. To further diversify the learned representation, \our{} injects self-similar fractal structure into the same salient regions at an adaptive ratio, so each training sample carries both fractal and non-fractal structure. We derive a second-order approximation of the resulting vicinal risk, showing that the method simultaneously enforces invariance to the generative edit and suppresses curvature along the perturbed salient directions, and we verify both predictions empirically. We evaluate on small to large backbones for instance Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs), Vision Transformers (ViTs) and Vision-Language Foundational Models (VLMs) across seven benchmarks covering coarse- and fine-grained classification, robustness to corruption and occlusion, calibration, and transfer and self-supervised learning, InstructMixup outperforms nine competing augmentation methods, surpassing the strongest baseline across all benchmarks.

</details>


### [75] [OmniReasoner: Thinking with Long Audio-Video via Native Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19339)
*Yu Chen,Caorui Li,Ziyu Xiong,Yidong Wang,Mingqi Gao,Shuman Liu,Biao Liu,Chunfeng Yang,Anxiang Zeng,Haibo Zhang,Chaofan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniReasoner 是一个面向长音频-视频推理的工具使用后训练框架，通过监督微调和强化学习让全模态大语言模型学会在回答前调用"缩放"工具对关键时空区域进行高保真检查，并引入 TimeAnchor 机制保证跨粒度的一致性，同时利用时序增强数据引擎合成训练轨迹，从而在降低计算成本的同时提升答案准确性和时序定位能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长音频-视频推理中，关键证据往往稀疏、跨模态，且难以在整个流上维持统一高保真输入，现有全模态大语言模型难以兼顾效率与精度。

Method: 提出 OmniReasoner 框架：(1) 工具使用后训练，通过 SFT + RL 让模型学会在回答前调用 zoom-in 工具；(2) TimeAnchor 机制，将工具的时间参数绑定到视频流时间而非帧索引，保证跨采样粒度的一致性；(3) 时序增强数据引擎，通过视频编辑和合成自动生成工具使用训练轨迹，避免昂贵的人工区间标注。

Result: 在全模态和视频基准测试上，OmniReasoner 在提高答案准确性和时序定位能力的同时，将高保真计算集中在信息丰富的区域，降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: 通过让模型自主决定何时何地调用高保真工具进行局部细粒度检查，可以有效解决长音频-视频推理中的稀疏跨模态证据问题，实现了效率与精度的平衡。

Abstract: Long audio-video reasoning is difficult for omnimodal LLMs because the decisive evidence is often sparse, cross-modal, and too expensive to preserve with uniformly high-fidelity inputs. We introduce OmniReasoner, a tool-use post-training framework for Thinking with Long Audio-Video: omni-modal LLMs learn, via supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning, to decide whether and where to call a zoom-in tool before answering. OmniReasoner first builds a low-cost global preview of the full stream and then, when needed, calls the zoom-in tool with a requested temporal interval for higher-fidelity visual and audio inspection before answering. Because the model observes different sampling granularities before and after this call -- a sparse global preview and a denser local clip -- we introduce TimeAnchor, which keeps the tool's temporal argument valid and round-trip-consistent across these granularities, rather than tied to frame indices from a particular sampling rate. To make this tool-use behavior trainable without expensive manual interval annotation, we build a Temporal Augmented Data Engine that synthesizes tool-use post-training trajectories by video editing and composition. Experiments across omnimodal and video benchmarks show that OmniReasoner improves both answer accuracy and temporal grounding while concentrating high-fidelity computation on informative regions. Code is available at https://github.com/RockyChen0205/OmniReasoner.

</details>


### [76] [ExpertVerse: A General-Purpose Benchmark for Expert-Level Reasoning in Knowledge-Intensive Visual Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19341)
*Yuan Wang,Yongchao Du,Mengting Chen,Jinsong Lan,Xuetao Feng,Xiaoyong Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ExpertVerse是一个评估多模态生成模型在知识密集型推理任务中表现的能力中心基准，包含9种认知能力×8个专家学科=58个子学科，共1,611个专家标注实例。同时提出了KnowThinker推理引擎和Bootstrapped Pareto Policy Optimization (BPPO)训练方法，以解决跨模态信用错配和多目标梯度冲突问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令驱动的图像生成方法仅关注显式常识推理、浅层因果理解和直接知识回忆，无法处理知识密集型生成任务，缺乏对生成模型知识推理能力的系统性评估基准。

Method: 构建ExpertVerse基准（9种认知能力×8个专家学科的正交分类体系，1,611个专家标注实例，涵盖单图编辑、多图组合和文生图三类任务）；开发ExpertVerse-100K大规模数据集（含推理轨迹和知识锚定标注）；提出KnowThinker（基于RL微调的VLM推理引擎，联合生成思考过程与精炼指令）；提出Bootstrapped Pareto Policy Optimization (BPPO)，包含Bootstrapping Reward Rectification (BRR)和Conflict-Aware Pareto Advantage Fusion (CPAF)两个组件。

Result: 开源和闭源模型在该基准上的表现均暴露出显著的推理缺陷，表明构建知识密集型基准对于下一代视觉生成模型至关重要。

Conclusion: 现有生成模型在知识密集型推理任务上存在严重不足，亟需面向知识密集型视觉生成的基准和方法；BPPO可以有效解决多奖励优化中的跨模态信用错配和梯度冲突问题。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal generative models have enabled instruction-based image generation to move beyond semantic manipulation to knowledge-driven visual reasoning. However, these methods focus on explicit commonsense reasoning, shallow causal understanding, and direct knowledge recall, failing at knowledge-intensive generation. We develop \textbf{ExpertVerse}, a capability-centric benchmark to evaluate generative models via knowledge-intensive lens. ExpertVerse stratifies reasoning generation across an orthogonal taxonomy of \textit{9 cognitive capabilities} and \textit{8 expert disciplines}, yielding \textit{58 sub-disciplines}. We curate 1,611 expert-annotated instances covering single-image editing, multi-image composition, and text-to-image generation. We further develop an automated workflow to produce \textbf{ExpertVerse-100K}, a large-scale dataset with reasoning traces and knowledge-anchored rationale annotations. Based on this, we train \textbf{KnowThinker} with RL fine-tuning, a VLM reasoning engine with world knowledge that jointly generates thinking processes and refined instructions. Towards the cross-modal credit misalignment and multi-objective gradient conflicts in multi-reward optimization, we propose a tailored Bootstrapped Pareto Policy Optimization (BPPO), which synergizes Bootstrapping Reward Rectification (BRR) and Conflict-Aware Pareto Advantage Fusion (CPAF). Extensive results of both open-source and proprietary models exposes critical reasoning deficits, highlighting imperative for knowledge-intensive benchmarks towards next-generation visual generation.

</details>


### [77] [Masked Visual Actions for Unified World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19343)
*Hadi Alzayer,Wenlong Huang,Haonan Chen,Christopher Luey,Lvmin Zhang,Maneesh Agrawala,Gordon Wetzstein,Li Fei-Fei,Yilun Du,Jiajun Wu,Jia-Bin Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Masked Visual Actions（MVA），一种像素空间控制接口，通过在视频中部分揭示任意实体的轨迹来表达动作，使视频模型兼具前向动力学、逆建模和模型预测规划能力，仅需15小时微调即可实现高质量视觉预测与控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频模型蕴含丰富的视觉世界交互先验，但如何将机器人动作以与视觉空间对齐的形式传递给这些模型，同时保证其与物理操作的一致性，是一个核心挑战。

Method: 提出Masked Visual Actions（MVA），将动作表示为视频中任意实体轨迹的部分揭示；通过遮蔽-揭示策略，使模型既能作为前向动力学模型预测场景对底层机器人动作的响应，也能通过揭示期望物体运动来逆向恢复机器人行为。仅需15小时的真实视频和模拟数据微调。

Result: 单个检查点在多种场景和多具身平台上实现了强视觉保真度和可控性；在操作任务中，模型生成的想象 rollout 结果与真实执行结果相关，可用于策略评估；通过排序候选未来轨迹改善模型预测规划决策；支持从期望物体运动合成机器人动作的逆建模。

Conclusion: MVA提供了一种轻量级、高效的方式将视频模型转化为机器人世界模型，在少量数据微调下实现了前向预测、逆建模和规划等多种能力，验证了像素空间动作表达作为机器人-视频模型接口的有效性。

Abstract: Video models absorb rich priors over how the visual world moves, interacts, and responds to contact, making them promising substrates for robotic world modeling. The central challenge is how to communicate action to such models in a form aligned with the visual space in which they learned these interaction priors, yet still grounded in physical manipulation. We introduce Masked Visual Actions, a pixel-space control interface that expresses action as a partially revealed trajectory of an arbitrary entity in a video. Revealing robot motion makes the model act as a forward dynamics model that predicts the scene's response to low-level robot actions, while revealing desired object motion makes the same model recover robot behavior consistent with that outcome. Finetuned with only 15 hours of masked examples from real videos and simulation, a single checkpoint achieves strong visual fidelity and controllability across diverse scenes and multiple embodiments. In downstream manipulation settings, the model produces imagined rollouts whose outcomes correlate with real-world execution for policy evaluation, improves decision making by ranking candidate futures in model-based planning, and supports inverse modeling by synthesizing robot motion from desired object motion.

</details>


### [78] [Appearance Pointers -- Multimodal Region Control of Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19344)
*Rahul Sajnani,Yulia Gryaditskaya,Radomír Měch,Srinath Sridhar,Matheus Gadelha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出外观指针（Appearance Pointers），一种用于扩散变换器（DiT）的模态无关接口，通过区域对应网络和空间聚合机制，在无需重新训练基础模型的情况下实现精确的区域级多模态可控图像生成，性能达到或超越现有模态特定方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创意专业人士需要精确的区域级控制（材质、物体身份、空间布局），但现有文本提示方法无法可靠实现；DiT虽能处理文本和图像的异构token，但缺乏控制这些token在何处以何种方式影响输出的机制。

Method: 提出外观指针（Appearance Pointers）——紧凑的引导token，通过区域对应网络（Region Correspondence Network）生成，并经过空间聚合机制（Spatial Aggregation Mechanism）细化，将文本或图像输入与用户指定的掩码对齐，指导DiT在正确空间位置产生正确外观线索，支持多区域描述而不显著增加token负载。

Result: 在多种评估指标上，单个模型达到或超越了模态特定的最先进方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出了首个面向DiT的模态无关局部多模态控制接口，无需重新训练基础模型，为生成式图像合成中精确、区域感知的多模态引导提供了一种简单且可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Controllable image generation remains challenging for creative professionals, who often require precise regional control over materials, object identities, and spatial arrangements that cannot be reliably achieved through text prompting alone. Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) can natively ingest heterogeneous tokens stemming from texts and images, but they lack mechanisms for determining where and how these tokens should influence the output. We introduce appearance pointers, compact tokens that guide DiTs toward the correct appearance cues at the correct spatial locations by aligning text or image inputs with user-specified masks. Appearance pointers are produced by a region correspondence network and refined through a spatial aggregation mechanism, enabling the model to handle multiple regional descriptions without significantly increasing token load. Our approach introduces the first modality-agnostic interface for localized multimodal control in a DiT without retraining the base model from scratch. Across a range of metrics, our single model reaches or surpasses the performance of modality-specific state of the art methods, offering a simple and extensible path toward precise, region-aware, multimodal guidance in generative image synthesis.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [79] [FALCON-Discover: Discovering Concentrated False-Confidence Regions for Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18278)
*Filippo Cenacchi,Longbing Cao,Runze Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FALCON-Discover框架，用于检测模型在局部区域中高度自信但预测错误的现象（虚假置信集中）。通过置信度、局部支持、邻域一致性和扰动稳定性产生的差异信号对预测进行排序，在七个表格数据集上发现该现象普遍存在但依赖于具体场景，差异排序优于传统校准基线，且最佳检测器因数据集而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统校准评估关注全局聚合指标，但最危险的失效通常发生在局部：模型在错误预测时仍保持高置信度。这种局部过度自信的失效模式被严重忽视，需要专门的检测方法。

Method: 提出FALCON-Discover，一个事后、模型无关的框架。利用置信度、局部支持（邻域密度）、邻域一致性和扰动稳定性四个信号生成差异分数，对预测进行排序以发现虚假置信集中区域。在七个二分类表格数据集上使用XGBoost和CatBoost等强学习器进行五折交叉验证。

Result: 虚假置信集中现象普遍存在但具有机制依赖性。在主置信阈值下，差异排名在最强机制中显著优于验证集选择的校准或信任评分基线，而原始置信度几乎无法捕捉危险错误。最佳检测器因数据集而异：学习型差异在需要组合多种信号时最强，稳定性排名在局部决策脆弱性主导时最佳。

Conclusion: 危险过度自信更适合作为家族级发现问题而非单分数校准问题处理，应设计明确针对置信度、支持度和稳定性分歧区域的校准策略。

Abstract: Calibration is usually evaluated in aggregate, but the most dangerous failures are often local: predictions that remain highly confident despite being wrong. We study this failure mode as false-confidence concentration, the extent to which confident errors occupy compact, discoverable regions of prediction space. We introduce FALCON-Discover, a post-hoc, model-agnostic framework that ranks predictions using discrepancy signals from confidence, local support, neighborhood agreement, and perturbation stability. Across seven binary tabular datasets, four seeds, five-fold cross-fitting, and strong learners including XGBoost and CatBoost, we find that false-confidence concentration is recurrent but regime-dependent. At the main confidence threshold, discrepancy-based ranking substantially outperforms the strongest validation-selected calibration or trust-scoring baseline in the strongest regimes, while raw confidence recovers little dangerous-error mass. The best detector varies across datasets: learned discrepancy is strongest when multiple cues must be combined, whereas stability-centered ranking works best when local decisional fragility dominates. These results show that dangerous overconfidence is better treated as a family-level discovery problem than as a single-score calibration problem, and motivate calibration strategies that explicitly target regions where confidence, support, and stability diverge.

</details>


### [80] [Beyond Output-Space Calibration: Spectral Evidence Bundling for Selective Reliability Estimation in Time-Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18279)
*Filippo Cenacchi,Longbing Cao,Runze Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种验证门控的固定标签可靠性策略，通过结合输出置信度与频域谱特征（如频带能量、熵、峰值优势、周期支持和相位稳定性）来评估时间序列分类器的预测可靠性，在保持原始预测不变的前提下决定是否信任该预测，实验表明该方法显著提升了可靠性指标（Corr-AURC从0.693提升至0.786），并降低了假高置信错误率（FalseConf@0.9降至0.094）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列分类的事后校准方法仅重映射输出分数，但部署决策（信任、弃权、审查）依赖预测是否得到当前时间信号的支持。现有方法存在三个缺陷：1)相同置信度值可能隐藏不同的时间支持；2)平均校准可能遗漏假高置信错误；3)输出空间重校准缺乏与输入关联的可审计性。

Method: 提出验证门控的固定标签可靠性策略：保留骨干网络预测不变，融合输出侧线索与全样本谱描述子（频带能量、熵、峰值优势、周期支持、相位稳定性）形成标量可靠性估计和诊断级频带证据；设置验证门控，仅当正确性排序提升且不破坏FalseConf@0.9或AURRC容差时才启用谱条件化，否则回退到输出空间基线。

Result: 在8个UCR/UEA数据集、8种时间序列骨干网络和标准重校准器上，无约束方法将Corr-AURC从0.693提升至0.779；验证门控策略进一步将Corr-AURC提升至0.786，并将FalseConf@0.9降至0.094。

Conclusion: 时间序列分类器的可靠性估计受益于将输出置信度与谱证据相结合，验证门控机制可防止无支持的谱条件化带来的负面影响。

Abstract: Post-hoc calibration for time-series classification usually remaps output scores, but deployment decisions such as trust, abstention, and review depend on whether a confident prediction is supported by the current temporal signal. We address three time-series reliability gaps: identical confidence values can hide different temporal support, average calibration can miss false high-confidence errors, and output-space recalibration offers limited input-linked auditability. We introduce a validation-gated fixed-label reliability policy that keeps the backbone prediction unchanged while estimating whether it should be trusted. The method combines output-side cues with whole-sample spectral descriptors, including band energy, entropy, peak dominance, period support, and phase stability, to form a scalar reliability estimate and diagnostic band-level evidence. A validation gate enables spectral conditioning only when correctness ranking improves without breaching FalseConf@0.9 or AURC tolerances; otherwise it reverts to the safer output-space baseline. Across eight heterogeneous UCR/UEA datasets, eight time-series backbone families, and standard recalibrators, the unconstrained method improves fixed-label selective-reliability metrics on the matched evaluation subset, raising Corr-AURC from 0.693 to 0.779. The validation-gated policy further improves Corr-AURC to 0.786 and reduces FalseConf@0.9 to 0.094. These results suggest that reliability estimation for time-series classifiers benefits from bundling output confidence with spectral evidence, while validation gating prevents unsupported spectral conditioning.

</details>


### [81] [Beyond Single-Dimensional Compression: The Compound Sparsity Frontier of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18280)
*Chao Han,Haozhe Hu,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合静态参数压缩（低秩近似+通道剪枝）和动态token级层跳过的复合稀疏框架，实验表明在相同总稀疏度下，复合压缩优于单一机制，且平衡分配最有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在压缩时，单一机制（静态参数剪枝或动态token级计算）超过一定稀疏度后性能会急剧下降，本文探索能否通过结合两种机制来分摊压缩负担、延缓性能退化。

Method: 先通过低秩近似和通道剪枝获得静态压缩骨干，再引入轻量级路由器实现逐token动态层跳过，从而独立控制参数稀疏度和token级计算稀疏度。

Result: 在理解任务上复合压缩延迟了性能衰减点，在建模任务上保持了更强的性能；发现参数剪枝和token跳过之间存在跨维度干扰，且在固定稀疏预算下平衡分配最有效。

Conclusion: 复合压缩是提升LLM压缩效率的实用方法，但存在更广泛的跨维度稀疏边界最终限制进一步压缩。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often compressed through static parameter pruning or dynamic token-level computation, yet aggressive sparsification can trigger rapid performance degradation beyond an essential sparsity boundary. This work asks \emph{whether combining these two mechanisms can delay such degradation by distributing the compression burden}. We study a minimalist compound sparsity framework that first applies low-rank approximation and channel pruning to obtain a statically compressed backbone, and then introduces lightweight routers for per-token dynamic layer skipping. This design enables independent control of parameter sparsity and token-level computation sparsity. Experiments across language understanding and modeling benchmarks show that compound sparsity consistently outperforms single-mechanism compression under the same total sparsity, delaying the decay point on understanding tasks and preserving stronger modeling performance. Further analysis reveals cross-dimensional interference between parameter pruning and token skipping, and shows that near-balanced allocation is most effective under a fixed sparsity budget. These results demonstrate that compound compression provides a practical way to improve LLM compression, while revealing a broader cross-dimensional sparsity boundary that ultimately limits further compression. Code will be available at https://github.com/EIT-NLP/LLM-Pruning.

</details>


### [82] [PAC--Bayes Bounds on Quotient Parameter Spaces: Geometry-induced Implicit-Bias Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18422)
*Nicola Aladrah,Fabio Anselmi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 过参数化模型存在连续参数对称性，本文提出应在商预测器空间进行PAC-贝叶斯分析，通过构建几何体积感知的隐式偏置先验，在保持风险不变的同时消除冗余KL散度，实验验证了该方法在傅里叶回归和注意力机制中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化模型中不同参数可定义相同预测器（参数对称性），导致PAC-贝叶斯分析的KL散度项包含冗余信息，影响了证书的紧致性。现有方法未充分利用参数几何结构来构造更优先验。

Method: 1) 将先验和后验推到商空间，消除参数化冗余带来的KL贡献；2) 为每个预测器构造典范参数化，并考虑其等效参数化的几何体积，将中性参考先验变换为反映模型隐式偏置的数据无关先验；3) 该先验近似理想但不可行的后验匹配先验。

Result: 傅里叶-哈达玛实验中，隐式偏置先验使商空间KL均值降低40.69%，PAC-贝叶斯证书均值降低21.40%。查询-键注意力实验中观察到较小的、依赖先验尺度的改进，证实了预测的条件性效应。

Conclusion: 商空间分析结合几何体积感知的隐式偏置先验能有效提升PAC-贝叶斯证书的紧致性，且改进程度取决于隐式偏置先验与学习后验之间的KL散度是否显著小于中性先验。

Abstract: Overparameterized models often have continuous parameter symmetries, so different parameters define the same predictor. We show that PAC--Bayesian analysis should be performed on the quotient predictor space: pushing a prior and posterior to the quotient preserves the empirical and population Gibbs risks while removing the nonnegative KL contribution caused solely by how the two distributions differ among parameterizations of the same predictor.
  Quotienting alone does not determine which prior to use. We construct a canonical choice of one parameterization for each predictor and account for the geometric volume of its equivalent parameterizations. This transforms a neutral reference prior into a data-independent prior that reflects the model's implicit bias. It approximates the ideal but inadmissible posterior-matched prior, which would minimize the KL term by depending on the training data. The resulting certificate is tighter exactly when this geometry-induced prior has smaller KL divergence from the learned quotient posterior than the neutral prior.
  We test this prediction in Fourier regression with a Hadamard parameterization and in Query-Key attention, using ordinary SGD without an explicit regularizer. The implicit-bias prior reduces the mean quotient-space KL by \(40.69\%\) and the mean PAC--Bayes certificate by \(21.40\%\) in the Fourier-Hadamard experiment. The smaller, prior-scale-dependent improvement in Query-Key attention confirms the predicted conditional nature of the effect.

</details>


### [83] [ALAS: Additive Learnable Alpha-Stable Kernels for Flexible Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18282)
*Weibo Huang,Cheng Hua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ALAS核函数族，通过对称α稳定谱成分构建灵活的高斯过程核，能够自适应学习数据的平滑度，同时捕捉平滑趋势和剧烈不规则性，并在多种基准测试中表现稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化的成功依赖于选择与目标函数未知结构相匹配的核函数，而传统方法难以同时适应不同平滑程度的函数。

Method: 基于对称α稳定谱成分构建高斯过程核函数族ALAS，通过学习稳定性参数α自适应调整有效平滑度；提出ALAS-Sep可分离变体，学习每个维度的尾行为以提升对近似可分解目标的鲁棒性。

Result: 在标准基准测试和真实代理模型上，ALAS及其变体在多种场景下展现出强且稳健的性能。

Conclusion: ALAS核函数族能够有效自适应数据平滑度，兼顾平滑趋势与剧烈不规则性，为贝叶斯优化提供了一种鲁棒的核选择方案。

Abstract: Bayesian Optimization is widely used for expensive black-box optimization, yet its success often depends on choosing a kernel that matches the objective's unknown structure. In this work, we propose ALAS, a flexible Gaussian Process kernel family built from symmetric $α$-stable spectral components. By learning the stability parameter $α$, ALAS adapts its effective smoothness from data, capturing both smooth trends and sharp irregularities. We present two parameterizations: ALAS, a single stationary component with joint spectral modulation, and ALAS-Sep, a separable variant that learns dimension-wise tail behavior to improve robustness on approximately decomposable objectives. Experiments on standard benchmarks and real-world surrogates demonstrate strong and robust performance across diverse settings.

</details>


### [84] [Elicitation without Backpropagation: Steering Model Behavior by Optimizing the Latent Posterior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18804)
*Garrett Baker,Vinayak Pathak,Daniel Murfet,Susan Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Posterior Prefix Tuning (PPT) 方法，利用贝叶斯滤波变换器（BFT）的潜在后验模型，通过仅从先验采样的梯度估计来优化提示分布，从而高效引导变换器生成高期望效用的续文，且无需额外前向或反向传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在变换器的潜在后验模型中，下一个词元的分布由基于上下文的潜在预测模型的后验决定。作者希望利用这一精确成立的情境（即贝叶斯滤波变换器），设计一种无需大量计算即可有效引导模型生成高效用续文的方法。

Method: PPT 方法优化硬提示上的分布参数。它先通过预测性蒙特卡洛（PMC）从 BFT 中一次性抽取先验样本，然后利用重要性采样从这些样本中估计目标函数（续文期望效用）的梯度。整个优化过程不经过变换器的前向或反向传播，且先验样本与效用函数无关，可复用于任意多个效用函数。

Result: 在 Beta-Bernoulli 和 reinforced urn 两种 BFT 上，针对逆向交叉熵、频率匹配和 Dyck 有效性三类效用函数进行验证，PPT 成功引导模型生成高期望效用的续文。

Conclusion: PPT 是一种高效且计算代价极小的提示优化方法，能够在贝叶斯滤波变换器上实现灵活的效用引导生成，具有良好的可复用性。

Abstract: In the \emph{latent posterior model} of transformer behavior, the next-token distribution arises from a posterior over latent predictive models conditioned on the context, mixed to generate continuations. We exploit this model in settings where it is exact, namely Bayes-filtered transformers (BFTs) meta-learned on sequences from a hierarchical prior, to introduce \textbf{Posterior Prefix Tuning (PPT)}, a new method for \emph{eliciting} behavior from a transformer: given a utility function on continuations, find a prompt under which the transformer generates continuations of high expected utility. For a BFT, the elicitation objective factors through the latent posterior, and the gradient of this objective can be estimated from samples of the prior alone. PPT optimizes the parameters of a distribution over hard prompts: it draws prior samples once from the BFT via predictive Monte Carlo (PMC), then estimates the gradient by importance sampling against them. The optimization performs no transformer forward passes and no backpropagation through the transformer, and the prior samples are utility-independent, so a single set of samples drives elicitation against any number of utilities at negligible marginal cost. We validate PPT on Beta--Bernoulli and reinforced urn BFTs across three utility families (reverse cross-entropy, frequency matching, Dyck validity).

</details>


### [85] [FedCC: A Low-Resource Federated Adaptation of Foundation Models for Robust Corpus Callosum localization in Fetal Ultrasound Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18283)
*Alessandro Di Matteo,Sara Moccia,Giuseppe Rizzo,Gianpaolo Grisolia,Ricciarda Raffaelli,Lorenzo Vasciaveo,Francesco D'Antonio,Maria Chiara Fiorentino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedCC，一个基于联邦学习的框架，用于在胎儿超声图像中定位胼胝体。该框架结合冻结的DINOv2骨干网络、轻量级YOLO检测头和LoRA低秩适配模块，在无需数据共享的多中心、资源受限临床场景下，以仅2.9M可训练参数（相比全微调降低8.5倍通信成本）实现了平均mAP@50为0.857、F1-score为0.803的性能，优于全微调和编码器冻结基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胎儿超声图像中胼胝体（CC）的准确定位对早期识别神经发育异常至关重要，但超声成像存在低对比度、斑点噪声和CC解剖变异大等固有局限性，且多中心临床场景面临数据隐私保护和资源受限的挑战。

Method: 提出FedCC联邦学习框架：1) 采用冻结的DINOv2作为视觉骨干网络提取通用特征；2) 使用轻量级YOLO检测头进行目标定位；3) 引入LoRA低秩适配模块实现参数高效微调，仅优化和交换少量参数，大幅降低计算与通信开销；4) 在联邦平均（FedAvg）策略下进行多中心协同训练。

Result: 在包含10,970帧超声图像（来自58名孕妇、3个临床中心、多种成像设备）的多中心数据集上，FedCC（DINOv2+LoRA+FedAvg）达到平均mAP@50为0.857、F1-score为0.803，优于全微调和编码器冻结基线；可训练参数仅2.9M（相比全微调24.4M），通信成本降低约8.5倍。

Conclusion: FedCC在联邦学习框架下实现了参数高效、通信节约且隐私保护的胎儿胼胝体定位，展现了向可扩展、隐私保护、临床可部署的胎儿神经超声AI系统迈进的潜力。

Abstract: Accurate localization of the corpus callosum (CC) in fetal ultrasound (US) images is crucial for the early identification of neurodevelopmental abnormalities. However, this task remains highly challenging due to the intrinsic limitations of US imaging, including low contrast, speckle noise, and the considerable anatomical variability of the CC. We propose FedCC, a federated learning (FL)-based framework for CC localization in fetal US images, specifically designed for realistic multi-center and resource-constrained clinical settings without requiring data sharing. The framework integrates a frozen DINOv2 backbone with a lightweight YOLO-based detection head. To enable parameter-efficient adaptation, Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) modules are incorporated, allowing only a small subset of parameters to be optimized and exchanged among clients. This strategy substantially reduces both computational and communication overhead, making the framework suitable for low-resource environments. The proposed approach was evaluated on a multi-center dataset comprising 10,970 ultrasound frames acquired from 58 pregnant women during routine neurosonographic examinations across three clinical sites using heterogeneous imaging devices. The proposed framework achieved strong performance in the federated setting. In particular, the combination of DINOv2 and LoRA under the FedAvg strategy achieved an average mAP@50 of 0.857 and an F1-score of 0.803, outperforming both full fine-tuning and encoder-freezing baselines. Notably, the proposed approach reduced the number of trainable parameters to 2.9M compared with 24.4M in full fine-tuning, corresponding to an approximately 8.5$\times$ reduction in communication cost. These findings represent a promising step toward scalable, privacy-preserving, and clinically deployable AI systems for fetal neurosonography.

</details>


### [86] [Deep learning-based prediction of time-resolved adhesive forces in viscoelastic Hertzian contacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19060)
*Ali Maghami,Merten Stender,Michele Ciavarella,Antonio Papangelo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习的序列到序列模型，用于快速预测粘弹性软接触中的粘附力响应。通过固定测量步长表示法和LSTM架构，模型在跨越四个数量级的加载速率和不同驻留时间的数据集上实现了高精度预测，中位推理时间仅0.16秒，可替代传统高成本数值模拟，适用于软体机器人和抓取控制场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统粘弹性软接触响应预测需要全数值模拟，计算成本高度依赖参数，难以满足实时应用或设计优化循环的需求。本文旨在通过深度学习模型克服这一限制，实现快速、准确的力轨迹预测。

Method: 采用序列到序列深度学习模型，引入固定测量步长（FMS）表示法将变长轨迹转换为定长序列；训练了LSTM、时序卷积网络（TCN）和时间分布密集层三种架构，并对比三种Tabor参数调节机制；数据集覆盖四个数量级的加载/卸载速率和不同驻留时间，Tabor参数范围0.2-3.2。

Result: 最优模型为带拼接调节机制的LSTM架构，留出集均方误差5.0×10⁻⁴，中位拉脱力误差约2.2%，中位滞后误差约1.1%，中位推理时间0.16秒。

Conclusion: 该模型可作为高成本数值模拟的快速替代方案，在未见参数组合和分析极限情况下均表现良好，具有控制导向应用的潜力。

Abstract: Fast prediction of the response of adhesive soft viscoelastic contacts represents a current challenge in soft robotics and for gripping and manipulation tasks. Determining the complete time-resolved force trajectory requires full numerical simulations, whose computational cost is strongly parameter-dependent, making them impractical for real-time application or design-optimization loops. In this work, we overcome this limitation by training a scalar-conditioned, stateful, sequence-to-sequence deep learning model to predict the full force evolution from a prescribed displacement history for both short- and long-range adhesion regimes. The data set spans four orders of magnitude in loading and unloading rates and includes varied dwell times, with the Tabor parameter ranging from $0.2$ to $3.2$. To enable learning across these heterogeneous time scales, we introduce a fixed-measurement-step (FMS) representation that converts variable-length trajectories into fixed-length sequences while preserving their physical-time information. Different architectures were trained, including long short-term memory (LSTM) networks, temporal convolutional neural (TCN) networks, and time-distributed dense layers with three different Tabor-conditioning mechanisms. The models were compared using global waveform and error metrics. We found that the best-performing model has an LSTM architecture with concatenated conditioning, which achieves a held-out mean-squared error of $5.0\times10^{-4}$, a median pull-off-force error of $\approx2.2\%$, and a median hysteresis error of $\approx1.1\%$. For the held-out protocols, the model predicts a complete force trajectory with a median inference time of $0.16$ s. The model is tested across unseen parameter combinations and against analytical limiting cases, providing a rapid surrogate for repeated numerical evaluations with potential use in control-oriented applications.

</details>


### [87] [Compressing What Matters: Neuron Importance Meets Data-Aware Low Rank Approximation for Language Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18284)
*Athanasios Ntovas,Alexandros Doumanoglou,Petros Drakoulis,Dimitris Zarpalas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了结合参数重要性和逐层功能等价性两种视角的SVD压缩方法，并设计了高效动态压缩率分配算法，在高压缩比下性能优于或持平现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型参数庞大，内存需求高，限制了在资源受限环境中的应用。现有SVD压缩方法要么从参数重要性角度出发，要么从逐层功能等价性角度出发，孤立研究这两种视角，且压缩率分配多采用均匀分配或计算昂贵的启发式搜索，缺乏高效且结合多视角的解决方案。

Method: 1) 将参数重要性与逐层功能等价性两种视角融合到一个统一的目标函数中进行低秩近似；2) 提出一种增强型且计算高效的动态压缩率分配算法，替代均匀分配或启发式搜索。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法在性能上与以往最先进方法持平或显著更优，尤其在高压缩比场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: 结合多视角的SVD压缩目标与高效动态压缩率分配策略是有效的，能更好地平衡压缩误差与下游任务性能。

Abstract: To excel at their domain large language models are comprised of billions of parameters. Yet this comes at the cost of huge memory requirements restricting their applicability in resource-constrained environments. To address the problem of neural network (NN) compression Singular Value Decomposition (SVD) has played a key role as a fundamental component for matrix compression through decomposition. To minimize compression error and to maximize the efficacy of the compressed model on the downstream tasks previous works focused on low-rank approximation of the NN's weight matrices either from the perspective of parameter importance or per-layer functional equivalence. While previous works studied the aforementioned perspectives in isolation in this work we are investigating the effectiveness of an approach that combines ideas from these two perspectives in a single objective. In parallel to this an important aspect that affects the compression quality is the distribution of the compression rate across layers and NN parameters. Earlier works mostly considered distributing the compression rate uniformly across layers and network weights or relied on computationally expensive heuristic search. Contrary to them in this work we propose an enhanced and computationally efficient algorithm for dynamic compression rate allocation. Experimental results support the efficacy of the proposed approach which performs on par or substantially better than the previous state-of-the-art especially under high compression ratios.

</details>


### [88] [Provable diffusion-based posterior sampling for linear inverse problems via DDIM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19333)
*Yuchen Jiao,Na Li,Changxiao Cai,Yuxin Chen,Gen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为PDDIM的算法，通过在标准DDIM更新中进行简单的逐坐标修正，将后验采样转化为协调式的DDIM更新，用于解决带扩散先验的线性逆问题。该方法根据观测信噪比与扩散信噪比的比较，在每奇异方向上切换扩散先验和基于测量的预测器，理论上证明收敛于贝叶斯后验，实验上在图像恢复任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散模型的逆问题后验采样器要么缺乏严格的理论保证，要么计算开销大，因此需要一种既高效又有理论保证的简单算法。

Method: 对标准DDIM更新进行轻量级的逐坐标修正，沿测量算子的每个奇异方向分别进行后验采样：当观测信噪比低于扩散信噪比时遵循学习到的扩散先验，否则切换到基于测量的校准预测器。

Result: 理论证明所提采样器收敛于以测量为条件的贝叶斯后验；实验上在多个图像恢复任务的大部分评估指标上取得最优性能。

Conclusion: 将带噪声线性逆问题的后验采样转化为简单的逐坐标DDIM更新，得到一个高效、易于实现且具有后验一致性的算法。

Abstract: Diffusion-based methods have achieved remarkable empirical success in solving inverse problems. However, many existing posterior samplers either lack rigorous theoretical guarantees or incur substantial computational overhead. We propose a simple and efficient algorithm, called \pddim, for solving linear inverse problems with diffusion priors via a DDIM-type sampler. Our method requires only lightweight, coordinate-wise modifications to the standard DDIM update, while explicitly incorporating the measurement model. The key idea is to perform posterior sampling separately along each singular direction of the measurement operator: for each direction, the sampler follows the learned diffusion prior when the observation signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) is below the corresponding diffusion SNR, and switches to a calibrated measurement-based predictor otherwise. We prove that the proposed sampler converges to the Bayesian posterior conditioned on the measurements. Empirical results show that the proposed sampler performs favorably against existing diffusion-based posterior samplers across a range of image restoration tasks, achieving the best performance on the majority of evaluation metrics considered. Overall, our results convert posterior sampling for noisy linear inverse problems to simple coordinate-wise DDIM updates, yielding an efficient, easy-to-implement algorithm with provable posterior consistency.

</details>


### [89] [Edge-Efficient Transformer for End-to-End RF Spectrum Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18285)
*Zhifan Song,Haralampos-G. Stratigopoulos,Hassan Aboushady*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了E-SpecFormer，一种用于自动调制识别和隐蔽信道检测的边缘高效Transformer模型，在RadioML2018和硬件木马隐蔽信道数据集上分别达到86.5%和94.2%的准确率，参数少于10k，每帧处理速度达92微秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型在射频任务中精度高但计算量大，难以部署在资源受限的物联网设备上，需要一种既高效又准确的小型化模型。

Method: 提出了E-SpecFormer架构，核心是LiTAN（线性Tanh注意力网络），该机制去除了Softmax和LayerNorm，降低复杂度同时提高射频任务精度。模型提供四种可扩展变体（Nano、Small、Medium、Large），适配不同硬件。

Result: 在RadioML2018数据集上，Nano变体在SNR>0 dB时达到86.5%平均准确率；在基于硬件木马的隐蔽信道数据集上达到94.2%准确率。模型参数少于10k，在FPGA/CPU协同执行下每帧处理速度达92微秒，超越现有边缘模型。

Conclusion: E-SpecFormer证明了在极低参数和延迟约束下实现高精度射频智能任务的可行性，为物联网设备上的实时频谱智能提供了边缘高效解决方案。

Abstract: We present E-SpecFormer (Edge Spectrum monitoring Transformer) for end-to-end automatic modulation and covert channel (CC) recognition. We introduce LiTAN (Linear Tanh Attention Network), a Softmax- and LayerNorm-free attention mechanism that reduces complexity while increasing accuracy in RF tasks. E-SpecFormer is parameterized in four scalable variants (Nano, Small, Medium, Large) to accommodate diverse hardware constraints. Using the RadioML2018 dataset for modulation recognition, the Nano variant achieves 86.5% average accuracy for Signal-to-Noise Ratios (SNRs)>0 dB, and on the hardware Trojan (HT)-based CC dataset it reaches 94.2% accuracy, both with fewer than 10k parameters and up to speed of 92 μs per frame on FPGA/CPU co-execution, surpassing state-of-the-art edge models at a fraction of their cost. These results establish E-SpecFormer as an edge-efficient solution for real-time spectrum intelligence on Internet of Things (IoT) devices. GitHub link to the repository: https://github.com/zsniko/E-SpecFormer.

</details>


### [90] [Preference-Conditioned Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning for Runtime-Tunable Transit Signal Priority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18286)
*Philip-Roman Adam,Stefanie Schmidtner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种偏好条件化的交通信号优先控制器，通过运行时调节偏好参数w在公交优先和整体交通延迟之间进行权衡，无需重新训练即可适应不同运营优先级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习的TSP方法通常优化固定奖励或固定标量化，无法灵活适应不同时段或扰动条件下机构优先级的变化，缺乏运行时调优的灵活性。

Method: 提出偏好条件化TSP控制器π(a|s,w)，在最小/最大绿灯时间和过渡可行性约束下选择下一信号相位，通过运行时偏好参数w调节公交优先与整体交通延迟的权衡。基于IntersectionZoo实现，扩展了带约束的信号控制/TSP封装，并采用公交流行度增强和基于时刻表的公交插入来应对训练中的稀疏公交优先事件。

Result: 单个学习到的条件化策略在运行时偏好上覆盖平滑的经验权衡前沿，优于固定时间和基于规则的基线，并保持约束可行性。尾部延迟诊断显示中等偏好设置下非公交外部性有限，但高公交优先权重下会大幅增加。

Conclusion: 偏好条件化方法能够在不重新训练的情况下实现不同运营优先级之间的灵活权衡，平衡公交延迟减少与非公交交通影响，为实际TSP系统提供了更具操作灵活性的解决方案。

Abstract: Transit signal priority (TSP) requires balancing competing objectives: reducing bus delay while limiting adverse impacts on non-bus traffic and avoiding extreme waits for a subset of vehicles. Existing reinforcement-learning (RL) approaches to TSP typically encode transit-aware features (e.g., occupancy and schedule deviation) but optimize a fixed reward or fixed scalarization, which limits operational flexibility when agency priorities change across time-of-day or disruption conditions. We present a preference-conditioned TSP controller, $π(a \mid s,w)$, that selects the next signal phase under minimum/maximum green and transition-feasibility constraints and can be tuned at runtime via a preference parameter $w$ to trade off bus-priority emphasis against overall traffic delay without retraining. We implement this on top of IntersectionZoo by introducing a constrained signal-control/TSP wrapper, and we extend scenario generation with bus-prevalence augmentation and timetable-based bus insertion to address sparse transit-priority events during training. Experiments against fixed-time control, a rule-based TSP overlay, and fixed-weight PPO specialists show that a single learned conditioned policy spans a smooth empirical trade-off frontier across runtime preferences, outperforms fixed-time and rule-based baselines, and maintains constraint feasibility, while tail-delay diagnostics reveal that non-bus externalities remain limited for moderate preference settings but can increase substantially under high bus-priority weights. The source code of this work is available at https://github.com/urbanAIthi/morl-tsp.

</details>


### [91] [BearingNAS: Obtaining In-Sensor Intelligent Fault Diagnosis Systems for Bearings Using a Laptop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18287)
*Andrea Mattia Garavagno,Edoardo Ragusa,Paolo Gastaldo,Antonio Frisoli,Rodolfo Zunino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BearingNAS，一种硬件感知神经架构搜索框架，用于在传感器芯片内进行轴承故障诊断。该框架针对极端微预算（4-8 KiB RAM，16-32 KiB Flash），采用无导数搜索策略和衰减核增长搜索空间，在笔记本电脑CPU上不到一小时完成搜索，在LSM6DSO16IS智能传感器处理单元上达到99.50%的诊断准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将机器学习推理能力从昂贵的外部GPU转移到传感器芯片内部，实现低功耗、低成本的边缘智能诊断，消除对离散GPU的依赖。

Method: 将神经架构搜索建模为约束优化问题，针对极端微预算；提出轻量级无导数搜索策略（无需GPU）；设计单数据流搜索空间，采用衰减核增长公式防止参数爆炸。

Result: 在笔记本电脑CPU上搜索收敛时间不到一小时；在CWRU轴承基准上，针对STMicroelectronics的三个目标（两个微控制器和LSM6DSO16IS ISPU）进行优化；最佳架构在ISPU上达到99.50%的诊断准确率。

Conclusion: 证明了将机器学习工作负载迁移到传感器封装内部的可行性，实现了低成本、可规模化的轴承故障诊断方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces BearingNAS, a Hardware-Aware Neural Architecture Search (HW-NAS) framework designed to shift the intelligence directly onto the sensor die via in-sensor processing. BearingNAS frames the search as a constrained optimization problem targeting extreme micro-budgets (4 to 8 kiB of RAM and 16 to 32 kiB of Flash). To eliminate the reliance on expensive discrete GPUs, we propose a lightweight, derivative-free search strategy paired with a single data-flow search space that leverages a decaying kernel growth formulation to prevent parameter explosion. We evaluate our framework on the Case Western Reserve University (CWRU) bearing benchmark, optimizing architectures for three STMicroelectronics targets: two commodity microcontrollers and the LSM6DSO16IS Intelligent Sensor Processing Unit (ISPU). Running entirely on a laptop CPU, the search converges in less than an hour. The resulting best in-sensor architecture achieves a highly competitive diagnostic accuracy of 99.50\% on the ISPU. These results demonstrate the viability of shifting the machine learning workload inside the sensor package, enabling low-cost, production-scale bearing fault diagnosis.

</details>


### [92] [Multi-Timescale Latent-Action DRL for Joint Optimization in Edge-Cloud Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18288)
*Vo Phi Son,Van-Dinh Nguyen,Ngoc Hung Nguyen,Trinh Van Chien,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对分层边缘云计算系统中的负载不平衡问题，提出了一种联合服务放置、计算委派和功率控制的两时间尺度多深度强化学习框架（2T-MDRL-LA），通过潜在动作空间压缩实现近最优的平均端到端延迟性能，相比无计算委派方案延迟降低20.8%，资源利用率提升13%，收敛速度比传统PPO快约50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分层边缘云计算系统中，动态任务到达和异构资源导致边缘与云层间负载不平衡，造成严重排队延迟和资源利用效率低下，现有方法难以有效解决这一挑战。

Method: 将JSCP问题分解为长期系统配置和短期资源分配两个子问题，提出两时间尺度多深度强化学习框架（2T-MDRL-LA），利用变分自编码器对高维组合动作空间进行潜在动作表示压缩，联合优化服务放置、用户关联、计算委派、任务卸载和用户发射功率。

Result: 该框架能有效适应动态网络条件，实现接近分支定界最优解的性能；相比无计算委派方案，平均端到端延迟降低20.8%，资源利用率提升13%；收敛速度比传统PPO快约50%。

Conclusion: 提出的2T-MDRL-LA框架通过两时间尺度分解和潜在动作空间压缩，有效解决了HECC系统中离散与连续变量强耦合导致的NP-hard优化问题，实现了动态环境下负载均衡和延迟性能的显著提升。

Abstract: Load imbalance across edge and cloud layers degrades latency performance in hierarchical edge-cloud computing (HECC) systems under dynamic task arrivals and heterogeneous resources, leading to severe queuing delays and inefficient resource utilization. To address this challenge, we study a joint service placement, computational delegation, and power control (JSCP) problem to minimize the average end-to-end (e2e) latency. The resulting JSCP problem is a mixed-integer nonconvex and NP-hard optimization problem due to the strong coupling between discrete and continuous variables. To enable tractable optimization and stable system adaptation, we exploit the inherent difference in decision dynamics and decompose the problem into long-term system configuration and short-term resource allocation subproblems. Based on this formulation, we propose a two-timescale multi-layer deep reinforcement learning framework with a latent action space (2T-MDRL-LA) to jointly optimize service placement, user association, computational delegation, task offloading, and user transmit power. A latent action representation based on a variational autoencoder is introduced to efficiently compress the high-dimensional combinatorial action space. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively adapts to dynamic network conditions and achieves near-optimal performance compared to branch-and-bound solutions. It achieves up to a 20.8% reduction in average e2e latency and a 13% improvement in resource utilization over the scheme without the computational delegation, while converging approximately 50% faster than conventional proximal policy optimization.

</details>


### [93] [Towards Principled Continual Anomaly Detection: A Systematic Framework and Benchmark Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18289)
*Kamil Faber,Mateusz Smendowski,Roberto Corizzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个系统化框架，用于从现有表格异常检测数据集中设计可重现的持续异常检测（CAD）基准场景，包括发现候选任务、过滤不合适任务以及推导能暴露多样动态的原则性排序，并基于三个网络安全数据集交付了五个基准场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格领域中任务边界很少给定，任意划分会导致不可学习、冗余或过度可迁移的任务，掩盖真正的持续学习行为，因此需要系统化的基准场景设计框架。

Method: 提出一个系统化框架，包括：（1）从现有表格异常检测数据集中发现候选任务；（2）过滤不合适的任务；（3）推导出能暴露多样动态的原则性排序，从而构建可重现的CAD基准场景。

Result: 基于三个大规模网络安全异常检测数据集，交付了五个基准场景，涵盖单数据集和多数据集两种CAD设置。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效构建高质量、可重现的CAD基准场景，解决任务定义、过滤、排序和验证等关键问题，为持续异常检测研究提供更可靠的评估基础。

Abstract: Continual anomaly detection (CAD) studies how models can adapt to evolving data distributions while retaining performance on previously observed regimes. CAD benchmarks, however, depend critically on how tasks are defined, filtered, ordered, and validated. In tabular domains, task boundaries are rarely given, and arbitrary splits can create unlearnable, redundant, or overly transferable tasks that obscure genuine continual-learning behavior. To this end, we introduce a systematic framework for reproducible benchmark scenario design from existing tabular anomaly-detection datasets. The framework discovers candidate tasks, filters unsuitable tasks, and derives principled orderings that expose diverse dynamics. The framework allows us to deliver five benchmark-ready scenarios from three large-scale cybersecurity anomaly detection datasets, yielding both single-dataset and multi-dataset CAD settings.

</details>


### [94] [SechKAN: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks with Hyperbolic Secant Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18290)
*Hoang-Thang Ta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SechKAN，一种基于双曲正割（sech）函数的KAN架构，利用其平滑钟形、局部响应和稳定梯度特性，在函数拟合、PDE问题和图像分类任务上优于MLP及其他KAN变体，且参数数量与MLP相当，但运行时间略长于MLP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KAN在机器学习和科学计算中表现出有效性，但现有KAN架构仍有改进空间，本文旨在设计一种更优的KAN架构以兼顾性能与模型规模。

Method: 采用双曲正割（sech）函数作为基函数，利用其平滑钟形、局部响应和稳定梯度特性；通过1D线性变换减少参数量，使模型规模与MLP相当。

Result: 在函数拟合、PDE问题以及MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100等图像分类任务上，SechKAN优于MLP和其他KAN变体，参数数量相近；运行时间优于其他KAN变体，但略长于MLP。

Conclusion: SechKAN是一种有效的KAN架构，在多项任务上展现优越性能，但推理速度仍有优化空间。

Abstract: In recent years, Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) have attracted increasing attention due to their effectiveness in machine learning and scientific computing tasks, offering a new paradigm for neural network design. In this paper, we present SechKAN, a KAN architecture based on hyperbolic secant (sech) functions. The hyperbolic secant basis is used for its smooth bell-shaped form, localized responses, and stable gradients. We employ 1D linear transformations to reduce the number of parameters, allowing SechKAN to remain comparable to multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) in model size. Experimental results indicate the effectiveness of SechKAN in function fitting, PDE problems, and image classification tasks on benchmark datasets, including MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100. SechKAN achieves superior performance compared to MLPs and other KAN variants while maintaining a similar number of parameters. However, its running time, while better than that of other KAN variants, is slightly longer than that of MLPs.

</details>


### [95] [Dual-domain fused LSTM modeling for efficient time-dependent reliability analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18291)
*Yixin Zhang,Mingyang Li,Zichao Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种双域融合长短期记忆（DDF-LSTM）模型，通过将时不变随机变量嵌入LSTM初始隐藏状态并设计改进损失函数，实现了高效准确的时间相关可靠性分析，四个案例验证了其计算效率和预测精度的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的代理模型方法难以纳入时不变随机变量并捕捉其时变随机过程之间的复杂交互作用，限制了时间相关可靠性分析的精度和效率。

Method: 提出DDF-LSTM模型：1) 将时不变变量嵌入LSTM初始隐藏状态；2) 引入全连接层同时映射LSTM输出和时不变变量到最终输出空间；3) 设计改进损失函数增强模型对最小响应的敏感性。

Result: 训练后的DDF-LSTM模型能够以最小计算成本进行高效蒙特卡洛模拟，估计时变失效概率；四个案例验证了该方法在计算效率和预测精度上的优越性。

Conclusion: DDF-LSTM模型有效捕捉了随机变量、随机过程与极限状态函数时间行为之间的依赖关系，为时间相关可靠性分析提供了一种高效准确的解决方案。

Abstract: Time-dependent reliability analysis is crucial for ensuring the long-term safety and performance of engineering systems under uncertainties. However, traditional surrogate model methods often struggle to incorporate time-independent random variables and capture their complex interactions with time-dependent stochastic processes. To overcome this limitation, this paper proposes a dual-domain fused long short-term memory (DDF-LSTM) model for efficient and accurate time-dependent reliability analysis. A novel network architecture is developed to jointly process information from both time-dependent and time-independent domains. Specifically, the time-independent variables are embedded into the initial hidden states, and a fully connected layer is introduced to map both LSTM outputs and time-independent variables into the final output space. Furthermore, an improved loss function is designed to emphasize the model's sensitivity to minimum responses, thereby improving the precision of failure probability estimation. The proposed method effectively captures the dependencies among random variables, stochastic processes, and the temporal behavior of limit state functions. Once trained, the DDF-LSTM model enables efficient Monte Carlo simulation to estimate time-dependent failure probabilities with minimal computational cost. Four case studies validate the proposed method's enhanced computational efficiency and predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [96] [Reliability Scales Inversely: Bigger Models Compound Mistakes Faster via a Hidden Auto-Regressive Risk Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18292)
*Kushal Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 大语言模型随规模扩大，知识差距缩小约6倍，但知识退化增长11-39倍；模型在低概率token上的雪球效应导致一种自信但不稳定的风险状态，该状态可被因果干预缓解35-74%，且无法被基于模型自身概率的检测器识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究用知识差距（更多数据、检索或规模）解释模型幻觉，但忽略了自回归模型固有的风险残余：模型一旦承诺一个低概率token，就会将其作为既定事实继续生成，导致错误雪球效应。本文旨在量化并解释这种随规模放大的风险。

Method: 定义每个位置的不一致度δ = log p_M - log p_O（模型与更强同族oracle的对数概率差），其二阶矩可分解为偏差²（KL散度平方）和风险（Var[δ]）。通过分析不同规模模型在该度量下的行为，并进行因果干预（on-policy、固定KL的方差收缩实验）。

Result: (i) 规模扩大时，知识差距下降约6倍，而知识退化增长11-39倍；(ii) 在虚构处，模型不确定性H(p_M)快速放松，但oracle参考风险持续长达17倍时间，形成连接连续虚构的自信但危险的风险状态（14B模型增加69%）；(iii) 因果干预使网络验证的幻觉减少35-74%；(iv) 基于p_M的检测器（如语义熵）在风险分支上检测率低约30%，而该分支包含近4倍更多的虚构。

Conclusion: 更大模型更快地雪球化错误，这种失效模式是主导性的、自我延续的、因果性的且对模型自身不可见的。规模提升能力的同时，系统性侵蚀了可靠性。

Abstract: As language models scale, answers start truer but degrade faster: scaling buys capability but erodes reliability. The knowledge-gap account - more data, retrieval, or scale - misses an auto-regressive risk residual that scale sharpens: the model commits to a low-probability token, conditions on it as established, and snowballs. We track this through per-position disagreement $δ= \log p_M - \log p_O$ against a stronger same-family oracle, whose second moment splits exactly into bias$^2$ $\mathrm{KL}(p_M \,\|\, p_O)^2$ and risk $\mathrm{Var}[δ]$. We present four findings: (i) under scaling, the knowledge gap falls $\approx$$6\times$ while knowledge degradation grows $11$-$39\times$; (ii) at a fabrication, felt uncertainty $H(p_M)$ relaxes quickly while oracle-referenced risk persists up to $17\times$ longer, leaving a confident-but-precarious risk regime that bridges consecutive fabrications ($+69\%$ at $14$B); (iii) this regime is causal - an on-policy, fixed-$\mathrm{KL}$ variance contraction cuts web-verified hallucination by $35$-$74\%$ across three model families; and, (iv) it structurally evades self-monitoring, with $p_M$-only detectors (e.g. semantic entropy) firing $\approx$$30\%$ less ($p<10^{-16}$) on the risky branch holding nearly $4\times$ more fabrications. Bigger models snowball mistakes faster, through a failure mode that is dominant, self-perpetuating, causal and invisible to the model itself.

</details>


### [97] [One Student, Many Teachers: Multi-Task On-Policy Distillation via Soft-Prompt Privileged Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18293)
*Yingzi Ma,Zichen Zhu,Ming Jiang,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于可学习软提示（Soft Prompt）的教师模型用于大语言模型的自蒸馏，在冻结骨干网络的情况下仅通过软提示区分教师与学生，避免了后验合理化与权重漂移问题，并支持多任务并行蒸馏，在四个下游任务上优于全参数微调且不损伤通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的同策自蒸馏（OPSD）方法中，教师要么通过输入注入特权上下文导致后验合理化，要么通过微调权重导致任务间漂移和遗忘。需要一种既不改变输入也不改变权重的教师设计。

Method: 教师与学生共享骨干网络，区别仅在于一个可学习的软提示（Soft Prompt）。该提示在冻结骨干的情况下用$(x, y_\text{gold})$对训练，生成任务特定教师，保留学生精确的表征几何。多任务场景下，每个样本路由到对应的软提示教师，并行吸收K个教师知识，推理时丢弃所有提示。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B-Base和Phi-4-mini-instruct上测试Science、Tool Use、Biology、Math四个任务，单任务变体匹配或超越全参数微调且训练参数量少数个数量级；多任务变体在Qwen3-1.7B-Base上平均56.2，达到最佳整体均值，同时保留通用能力基准，优于顺序SFT。

Conclusion: 基于软提示的教师蒸馏方法有效解决了传统OPSD中教师设计的缺陷，兼具高效性、可扩展性和通用能力保留优势。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) teaches large language models new skills through a teacher that shares the student's backbone and supervises its own rollouts. Existing teachers either inject privileged context at the input -- inducing post-hoc rationalization -- or fine-tune weights, accumulating drift and forgetting across tasks. We propose \method, whose teacher differs from the student only by a learnable soft prompt: trained on $(x, y_\text{gold})$ pairs with the backbone frozen, the prompt yields a task-specific teacher that preserves the student's exact representational geometry. \method\ extends naturally to multi-task settings by routing each example in a merged corpus to its corresponding soft-prompt teacher, allowing a single student to absorb knowledge from $K$ teachers in parallel; at inference, all prompts are discarded. On Qwen3-1.7B-Base and Phi-4-mini-instruct across four tasks (Science, Tool Use, Biology, Math), the single-task variant (OPD with a PT teacher) matches or exceeds full fine-tuning while training orders of magnitude fewer parameters, and the multi-task variant achieves the best overall average ($56.2$ on Qwen3-1.7B-Base) while preserving general-capability benchmarks -- in contrast to sequential SFT, which degrades both.

</details>


### [98] [Uncertainty Quantification for AI-Driven Crash Simulation Surrogates: A Comparative Study of Monte Carlo Dropout and Deep Ensemble on Open-Source Bumper Beam Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18294)
*Sudeep Chavare*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了蒙特卡洛Dropout（结合具体Dropout）和深度集成两种不确定性量化方法在工程代理模型中的应用，以汽车碰撞仿真为案例。结果表明，通过具体Dropout实现的无超参数、良好校准的不确定性估计，能以极低计算成本挑战深度集成作为黄金标准的地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程安全关键场景中，代理模型缺乏不确定性估计导致工程师无法判断模型何时不可信，现有不确定性量化方法缺乏系统性的头对头比较。

Method: 在NVIDIA PhysicsNeMo构建的开源代理流水线上，对蒙特卡洛Dropout（采用具体Dropout自动学习丢弃率）和深度集成两种方法进行头对头比较，以汽车钢制保险杠碰撞仿真为基准，从点预测精度、不确定性带校准度和计算成本三方面评估。

Result: 两种方法在精度与校准之间存在根本性权衡，挑战了深度集成作为不确定性量化黄金标准的普遍假设；具体Dropout能以深度集成的一小部分计算成本实现良好校准、无需超参数的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 在开源工程工作流中，可以实现无需手动调参且校准良好的不确定性估计，且计算成本远低于集成方法，为代理模型在安全关键工程中的可信应用提供了新路径。

Abstract: Machine learning surrogate models are increasingly being explored in engineering product development to augment simulation-driven design, offering near-instantaneous predictions that complement computationally expensive high-fidelity analyses. However, a critical gap limits their adoption in safety-critical workflows: a point prediction without an accompanying uncertainty estimate cannot tell an engineer when the model should not be trusted. This work presents a systematic, head-to-head comparison of two widely used uncertainty quantification approaches -- Monte Carlo Dropout and Deep Ensembles -- applied to an open-source surrogate pipeline built on NVIDIA PhysicsNeMo. A key contribution is the use of concrete dropout, a built-in PhysicsNeMo capability that eliminates the dropout rate as a manual hyperparameter by learning it end-to-end during training, directly addressing the most common criticism of Monte Carlo Dropout-based uncertainty quantification. Automotive crash simulation is used as the application domain, with a steel bumper beam impact problem serving as the benchmark. Both methods are evaluated on identical held-out simulations and compared on point accuracy, uncertainty band calibration, and computational cost. The results reveal a fundamental trade-off between accuracy and calibration that challenges the common assumption that deep ensembles are the default gold standard for surrogate uncertainty quantification. The findings demonstrate that well-calibrated, hyperparameter-free uncertainty estimates are achievable within a fully open-source engineering workflow at a fraction of the computational cost of ensemble approaches.

</details>


### [99] [On the Limits of Support-Preserving Alignment and Bounded Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18295)
*Aryan Dutt,Rui Mao,Anupam Chattopadhyay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在大型语言模型中，对齐策略结合有界安全过滤能否将有害行为概率降至零。通过形式化支持保留的对齐算子与有界过滤算法，并从计算与信息论角度论证有界过滤无法消除基模型分布支持的所有有害输出。实验表明，随着过滤计算量增加，有害输出率持续下降但始终高于零，存在经验性的有害下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明基于偏好的对齐无法完全消除有害行为，且外部过滤在最坏情况下计算困难，但尚不清楚实际对齐流程能否彻底消除有害行为而非仅抑制其最明显形式。本文旨在探究这一问题。

Method: 形式化定义了支持保留对齐算子与有界过滤算法，在黑盒、白盒和统计查询三种访问模式下分析其逼近理想消除器的能力；结合计算与信息论论证；并对多种开源及托管LLM进行实证评估。

Result: 理论和实证结果均表明，有界过滤无法完全消除基模型分布支持的所有有害输出；有害输出率随过滤计算量增加而下降，但始终在零以上存在平台期。

Conclusion: 在给定约束下，对齐策略结合有界安全过滤无法将LLM的有害行为概率降至零，存在固有的有害下限。

Abstract: We study whether alignment schemes that reshape a base model's output distribution, combined with bounded safety filters, can drive the probability of harmful behavior to zero in modern large language models. Recent research suggests that harmful behaviors can persist under preference-based alignment and that external filtering can be computationally hard in the worst case, but it remains unclear whether practical alignment pipelines that largely preserve internal representations can eliminate harmful behavior entirely rather than merely suppressing its most visible forms. We formalize this setting using support-preserving alignment operators together with bounded filtering algorithms under black-box, white-box, and statistical-query access, and analyze their ability to approximate an ideal eliminator that removes all harmful mass. Building on this framework, we provide computational and information-theoretic arguments indicating that, under these constraints, bounded filtering may fail to eliminate all harmful outputs supported by the base model's distribution. To evaluate these limits empirically, we analyze a range of state-of-the-art open-weight and hosted LLMs accessed via OpenRouter under bounded black-box, white-box, and statistical-query filters on adversarial prompts drawn from curated cybersecurity scenarios and PKU-SafeRLHF. Across models, filter classes, and query budgets, the estimated harmful-output rate decreases with additional filtering compute but consistently plateaus above zero, suggesting a persistent empirical harm floor.

</details>


### [100] [A Better Start for Language Models: Domain-Conditional Position Offsets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18302)
*Ye Qiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出领域条件位置偏移（domain-conditional position offset），通过向序列起始位置的嵌入激活添加单个学习向量，在不修改模型权重的情况下，显著降低自回归语言模型在序列开头的冷启动惩罚（perplexity降低高达27%），且训练仅需约100篇文档、数分钟，可在领域间热切换，无延迟开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归语言模型在序列起始位置因缺乏上下文而依赖预训练先验，导致准确率最低（冷启动惩罚），且该惩罚具有领域依赖性。现有方法如LoRA参数过多、soft prompt增加序列长度，因此需要一种轻量级、可热切换的领域适配方案。

Method: 在冻结全部模型权重的情况下，向序列前几个位置的嵌入激活添加一个可学习的领域条件位置偏移向量。该偏移量在约100篇文档上训练数分钟，不同领域之间切换无需维护序列状态，推理时无延迟开销。

Result: 在Mamba、GPT-NeoX、Llama系列模型（410M到8B参数）上，held-out领域perplexity降低高达27%；70B模型效果仍存在；单个位置即可捕获大部分收益。对比方法：直接logit偏置修正仅达7.9%且不影响后续token损失，LoRA参数多2-3个数量级且需低秩权重路径，soft prompt增加序列位置。在检索重排序和领域分类任务上也有提升，而少样本推理不受影响。

Conclusion: 该偏移量不是最强的适配器，但是一种轻量级、可热切换的工具，适用于短序列的领域内评分与校准。位置感知的prefill应用可辅助生成任务，而在每步缓存解码中朴素应用会导致重复。

Abstract: Autoregressive language models are least accurate at the beginning of a sequence, where little context forces reliance on a generic pretraining prior. We show that this cold-start penalty is domain dependent and reduce it with a domain-conditional position offset: a single learned vector added to the embedding activation at the first sequence positions while all model weights remain frozen. The offset trains in minutes on roughly one hundred documents, switches between domains without added sequence state, and has no measurable latency overhead. Across eight Mamba, GPT-NeoX, and Llama models spanning 410M to 8B parameters, it reduces held-out in-domain perplexity by up to 27%; the effect persists at 70B, and one position captures most of the benefit. A matched, converged direct logit-bias correction reaches at most only 7.9% and leaves later-token loss unchanged, showing that the offset propagates through model state rather than merely recalibrating the output prior. A tuned LoRA reaches lower perplexity but uses two to three orders of magnitude more parameters and an active low-rank weight path, while soft prompts add sequence positions. With wrong-domain controls, offsets improve retrieval reranking and domain classification when decisions depend on early in-domain tokens, For the few-shot reasoning whose signal occurs later, the results maintains unchanged. Position-aware prefill application also help generation tasks, whereas naive application at every cached decoding step causes repetition. The offset is therefore not the strongest adapter, but a lightweight, hot switchable tool for short in-domain scoring and calibration.

</details>


### [101] [TD-DPO: Difference-Aware Preference Optimization for Mitigating Sycophancy in Clinical Autism Intervention Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18304)
*Shuzhong Lai,Junhong Lai,Chenxi Li,Qing Zhou,Haifeng Li,Gang Pan,Lin Yao,Yueming Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对自闭症儿童干预对话中大语言模型的谄媚问题，提出MEDA数据增强策略和TD-DPO偏好优化方法，通过最小编辑构建偏好对并差异化优化token权重，在减少谄媚的同时保留干预能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在自闭症儿童干预对话中存在的谄媚行为会增加安全风险，现有监督微调方法仅依赖正例难以识别和纠正失败模式，序列级偏好优化会过度更新非偏好相关token并降低干预能力。

Method: 提出MEDA（最小编辑数据增强）策略构建受控、稳定、最小编辑的偏好对，以及TD-DPO（Token级差异直接偏好优化），该算法对选择响应和拒绝响应之间的差异token赋予更高权重，同时降低共享token的权重以抑制背景漂移。

Result: 在多个 backbone 和评估器上的实验表明，TD-DPO 在离线设置下实现了谄媚缓解与干预能力保持之间的更好权衡。

Conclusion: TD-DPO作为一种实用的对齐方法，在自闭症干预场景中具有潜力。

Abstract: The sycophancy of large language models can increase the safety risk in intervention dialogue for autistic children. Supervised fine-tuning can somewhat reduce sycophancy, but relying solely on positive examples is often insufficient to identify and correct failure patterns. We observe that sycophancy behaviors can often be localized to a limited span within the model response. In this regime, sequence-level preference optimization can over-update preference-irrelevant tokens and degrade intervention ability. To address this, we propose the \textbf{M}inimal \textbf{E}dit \textbf{D}ata \textbf{A}ugmentation (MEDA) strategy to construct controlled, stable, minimal edit preference pairs and \textbf{T}oken-level \textbf{D}ifference \textbf{D}irect \textbf{P}reference \textbf{O}ptimization (TD-DPO), which upweights difference tokens between chosen and rejected responses while downweighting shared tokens to suppress background drift. Extensive experiments across multiple backbones and evaluators show that TD-DPO achieves a better trade-off between sycophancy mitigation and intervention ability retention in our offline settings, highlighting its potential as a practical alignment approach for autism intervention.

</details>


### [102] [The Information Shadow: Measuring Structural Limits on What Language Models Can Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18305)
*Priyansh Srivastava,Romit Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"信息阴影"概念，指出语言模型的知识局限并非数据覆盖不足，而是从文本中学习的结构性属性。信息阴影包含三类：语言无法表达的结构、统计不可识别函数、以及可表示但梯度训练无法达成的函数。通过可验证的探针实验证明这三类阴影的存在且无法通过扩大数据规模解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示语言模型的知识局限并非源于数据覆盖不足，而是一种结构性的、无法通过扩大规模克服的根本性缺陷。

Method: 定义了信息阴影的三种类型，并为每种类型设计了可验证的探针测试：(I) 语言压缩残差——对比文本学习器与全信号学习器的性能差异；(II) 反事实区分测试——在两种不相容规则一致的数据上训练模型，观察其反事实行为；(III) 盆地逃逸映射——展示可表示但标准训练无法达成的函数。

Result: 1. 文本学习器存在可计算的表达上限，而全信号学习器在数据增加300倍后差距依然持平，证明缺陷源于通道而非训练；2. 模型反事实行为由归纳偏差决定，仅5%消歧数据即可双向引导学习规则（r=±1.0, p<1e-10）；3. 可表示函数标准训练达到0%，而从临近初始化瞬间可达，宽度缩放无帮助（p=1.6×10^-14）。

Conclusion: 语言模型存在无法通过增大数据规模或模型容量克服的固有限制，这些发现对基准设计、能力审计和不确定性量化具有重要启示。

Abstract: Some limits on what language models know are not gaps in data coverage but structural properties of learning from text. We introduce the information shadow: the region of phenomena that a text-trained learner cannot acquire regardless of scale, comprising (I) structures language cannot express, (II) functions that are statistically non-identifiable from the training distribution, and (III) functions that are representable but unreachable by gradient-based training. We give each type a probe that is decisive because the premise of the shadow is, in that setting, provable. For Type I, Language Compression Residuals compare a text learner, which sees only a lossy text-like encoding of the signal, against a full-signal learner, which sees the underlying signal directly. The text learner sits at a computable expressibility ceiling while the full-signal learner pulls away by a gap that stays flat across 300x more data, so the deficit is a property of the channel, not of training. For Type II, the Counterfactual Distinction Test trains models on data exactly consistent with two incompatible rules. Across a provable string task and a language-like agreement task, behavior on counterfactuals is set by the model's inductive bias, while 5% disambiguating data steers the learned rule bidirectionally to either target (r = +/-1.0, p < 1e-10). For Type III, Basin Escape Mapping exhibits a function that is representable at 100% (by hand construction) yet reached 0% of the time by standard training and instantly from a nearby initialization, with width scaling providing no help (p = 1.6 x 10^-14). Each effect is isolated by a control that rules out a capacity or modality artifact. We release the probe suite and discuss implications for benchmark design, capability auditing, and shadow-aware uncertainty.

</details>


### [103] [Gradient-Energy Guided Block-Wise Perturbations for Sharpness-Aware Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18306)
*Zhen Huang,Jiaxin Deng,Junbiao Pang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GEAR-SAM通过维护梯度平方的指数移动平均作为敏感性信号，利用闭式约束优化在参数块间动态分配SAM的固定扰动预算，在无需额外计算开销的前提下提升泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SAM在参数块间隐式地按瞬时小批量梯度范数分配全局扰动预算，这种分配噪声大且无法反映块在训练过程中累积的敏感性，导致扰动分配不够合理。

Method: 维护块级梯度平方的指数移动平均(EMA)作为轻量级的曲率相关敏感性信号；通过闭式约束优化在块间重新分配SAM的固定扰动预算；保持全局SAM半径不变，无需Hessian-向量积或显式Fisher估计。

Result: 在图像分类、迁移学习、噪声标签学习及分区研究等实验中，GEAR-SAM在多种架构和任务上均提升了泛化性能和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 固定的扰动预算应随着训练过程中功能网络块敏感性的演化而动态重新分配，这为锐度感知优化提供了动态视角。

Abstract: Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) improves generalization by minimizing the worst-case loss in a local parameter neighborhood. Standard SAM implicitly allocates its global perturbation budget across parameter blocks according to instantaneous minibatch gradient norms. Such an allocation can be noisy and may not reflect the sensitivity that blocks accumulate throughout training. We propose Gradient-Energy Adaptive Radius SAM (GEAR-SAM), which maintains an exponential moving average (EMA) of squared block gradients as a lightweight, curvature-related sensitivity signal and allocates the fixed SAM budget through a closed-form constrained optimization. GEAR-SAM preserves the global SAM radius, requires no Hessian-vector products or explicit Fisher estimation, and adds only scalar state beyond SAM. Experiments on image classification, transfer learning, noisy-label learning, and partition studies demonstrate improved generalization and robustness across architectures and tasks. More broadly, GEAR-SAM provides a dynamic view of sharpness-aware optimization: a fixed perturbation budget should be redistributed as the sensitivity of functional network blocks evolves during training.

</details>


### [104] [Agentic Calibration of Grey-Box Simulation Models: An LLM-Driven Alternative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18308)
*David Gómez-Guillén,Mireia Diaz,Josep Lluis Arcos,Jesús Cerquides*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的智能体校准方法，用于灰盒仿真模型的参数优化。该方法将约束条件以自然语言形式嵌入系统提示中，避免了传统贝叶斯优化所需的复杂建模。在肛门癌仿真模型上，该方法在无约束和有约束校准任务中均达到了与贝叶斯优化相当或更优的误差水平，且所需模型评估次数更少。主要代价是每次迭代的推理时间增加，但约束处理几乎零成本，且搜索过程具有可审计性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灰盒仿真模型的校准是一个受约束的高维优化问题，模型评估昂贵且参数间联合效应难以解析预测。经典优化器（如Nelder-Mead）样本效率低，贝叶斯优化虽高效但需复杂建模来处理约束，亟需一种既简单又高效的校准方法。

Method: 提出智能体校准方法：以大语言模型作为优化器，将约束条件以纯自然语言形式写入系统提示（system prompt），无需额外建模。在肛门癌仿真模型上，与Nelder-Mead（NM）和贝叶斯优化（BO）在无约束和临床约束两种场景下进行对比评估。

Result: 无约束校准中，智能体方法以更少的模型评估次数达到比BO和NM更低的误差；有约束校准中，智能体方法与BO误差相当，两者均优于NM。代价是每次迭代的推理时间增加，但约束处理通过文本描述实现，几乎零建模成本。

Conclusion: 智能体校准方法以较少模型评估达到竞争性能，约束处理通过简单文本规范即可实现，无需额外建模机制。主要权衡在于每次迭代的推理成本较高，因此特别适用于仿真时间占主导的场景。此外，每步推理过程可审计、可解释，便于第三方审查和验证。

Abstract: Calibration of grey-box simulation models is a constrained optimization problem in which model evaluations are expensive, the parameter space can be high-dimensional, and the search must respect plausibility constraints. Although the simulation code is fully available to the analyst, the joint effect of multiple parameters remains difficult to predict analytically. Classical optimizers such as Nelder--Mead (NM) are simple to deploy but sample-inefficient, particularly under constraints. Modern Bayesian Optimization methods achieve competitive solutions with far fewer evaluations but require non-trivial modeling machinery for constraint handling. We introduce an agentic calibration method in which a large language model acts as the optimizer, with constraints incorporated as a plain-language section of the system prompt. We evaluate the agentic method, NM, and Bayesian Optimization (BO) on an anal cancer simulation model under both unconstrained and clinically constrained calibration. Under unconstrained calibration, the agentic method achieves substantially lower best error than BO and NM, while requiring fewer model evaluations. Under constrained calibration, the agentic method reaches comparable error levels and both outperform NM. These results are obtained at the cost of increased inference time per iteration.
